# All vocal and live mic-path multi-effects — comparison

[Guitar](ALL-GUITAR.md) · [Bass](ALL-BASS.md) · [Vocal](ALL-VOCAL.md) · [Category](MULTI-EFFECTS.md) · [Repository](README.md)

All 68 recorded vocal or live mic-path multi-effects: 24 vocal-only and 44 with independent acoustic/general-instrument processing. Guitar-thru/chord detection is not a processed guitar path. FV01 remains excluded in [SINGLE-VOCAL.md](SINGLE-VOCAL.md); a mic input does not imply harmony or pitch correction.

These are **comparisons of the recorded catalogue**, not exhaustive market coverage or fresh stock certification. Organized 2026-10-16 from the linked source records; their original technical/price dates, firmware, revision and access limits still apply. Every model remains partial. **? means unverified, not No.** Read the complete linked record for sources and conflicting evidence; this view does not silently resolve those conflicts.

K/F/B = independently adjustable rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe and power switches are additional unless explicitly stated. Body axes and mass qualifications are retained. Color does not prove touch; USB audio does not prove bus power; IRs, native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary captures differ.

Checked2026-10-18 from the identity-matched2012 manual copy. — Evidence: [Manufacturer manual copy](https://www.scribd.com/document/467271239/VE-5-OM)<br>**Harmony / part evidence:** Own manual p6 describes **one added harmony part** above the singer. Do not borrow VE-20’s two-part architecture or VE-500’s three-part architecture. Manual p7 demonstrates changing the interval from third to fourth and independently adjusting harmony Gender. Full table’s −1 to +1 octave range is recorded above; intermediate menu steps remain unenumerated. — Evidence: [Manufacturer manual copy pp6–7](https://www.scribd.com/document/467271239/VE-5-OM)<br>**Synth / acoustic:** Pitchcorrection,harmony,Distortion/Radio/Strobe;instrumentsynth/acousticsimulation unverified. — Evidence: [Product](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ve-5/) | **IR / NAM / capture:** UserIR,native/convertedNAM,capture import unverified. — Evidence: Unresolved |
| [BOSS GT-001](BOSS.md#gt-001) | **Processing / choices:** Dual amps, series/parallel chains; COMP, PREAMP, OD/DS, EQ, FX1/2, DELAY, CHORUS, REVERB, PEDALFX, NS1/2, ACCELFX. Aggregate ceiling/category counts pending.<br>**Processing / preamp and drive:** Counting the [model-specific published lists](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-001/) gives **28 PREAMP choices** and **23 OD/DS choices**, including each list’s Custom entry. These are alternatives, not 28 simultaneous amps or 23 drive blocks.<br>**Processing / FX1-FX2 choices:** **35 distinct top-level choices across both slots**; Overtone and Tera Echo are FX2-only, so **FX1 has 33 / FX2 has 35**. This is a union count, not 35 choices in each slot or 35 simultaneous effects. Nested choices within algorithms remain uncounted.<br>**Processing / nested drive restriction:** A-DIST is available in the main OD/DS block and the Sub OD/DS algorithm of FX2; do not assign it to FX1’s Sub OD/DS. Aggregate simultaneous ceiling and remaining category counts still unverified.<br>**Processing / reverb:** P27 enumerates **seven**: Ambience, Room, Hall1, Hall2, Plate, Spring, Modurate (source spelling). Time 0.1–10 s; pre-delay 0–500 ms. Choices, not seven blocks.<br>**Processing / chorus:** P27 gives **three modes**: Mono, Stereo1, Stereo2; not three independent chorus slots.<br>**Processing / delay conflict:** P27 names **ten delay types**, but encodes TYPE as 00–0A (11 values); the eleventh is unnamed. Preserve discrepancy. Main time 1–2000 ms; dual sections 1–1000 ms; Sub Delay separately 1–1000 ms.<br>**Processing / nested compressor:** P19/23 enumerate **eight Advanced Comp subtypes** inside FX1/FX2; not eight extra processing slots or evidence that main COMP has eight types.<br>**Processing / wah:** [Own parameter guide, indexed p. 20](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf) lists **six wah types**: Cry, VO, Fat, Light, 7String and Reso. These are alternative sounds within the wah effect, not six simultaneous blocks. | **Factory / user memory:** 200 preset + 200 user; initial user contents unknown.<br>**Factory / memory reset:** [Official owner’s manual, indexed p. 12](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_OM.pdf) allows reset selection spanning system parameters, User Quick Settings and user patches U001–U200. WRITE prepares the reset and ENTER executes it; EXIT cancels beforehand. Selected saved settings are overwritten; back up needed data through the dedicated software. This does not prove every initial user slot contains a unique factory sound. | **Synth / acoustic:** WAVE SYNTH, AC. GUITAR SIM, AC. PROCESSOR listed; monophonic guitar-to-MIDI uses a normal pickup. | **IR / NAM / capture:** User IR, native/converted NAM, capture unverified. |

[Manufacturer history](https://www.boss.info/id/promos/vocal/);[French manual](https://fr.scribd.com/document/644989539/Boss-SE-50-fr)<br>**Processing / category choices:** [Roland catalog museum,NAM-1486,p11/PDFindex10](https://www.roland.com/jp/lp/catalog_museum/pdf/NAM-1486.pdf) identifies **19 effect types arranged into28 algorithms**. Exact chain membership/concurrency remains unresolved.<br>**Category choices / reverb7:** Hall1,Hall2,Room,Plate,Ambience,Gate Reverb,Stereo Reverb.<br>**Category choices / delay3:** Multi Delay,Multi Tap Delay,Stereo Delay.<br>**Category choices / other5:** Space Chorus,Multi Pitch Shifter,Stereo Pitch Shifter,Stereo Flanger,Stereo Phaser.<br>**Category choices / multi9:** Vocoder,Rotary,Keyboard Multi1,Keyboard Multi2,Rhodes,Guitar Multi,Vocal Multi,Stereo Enhancer,2CH Mixer.<br>**Category choices / paired4:** Reverb1+Reverb2,Gate Reverb+Reverb,Chorus+Reverb,Delay+Reverb.<br>**Processing / Rotary controls:** Separate low/high slow/fast rates andtransition times;Drive,level andseparation controls. **SPEED(TRIG)** toggles speed from a momentary MIDI controller;**SPEED** changes it while held. This does not establish an analog expression jack.<br>**Processing / Keyboard Multi1 limits:** EQ,delay,Super Chorus andreverb have documented parameters;delay **0–800ms**,reverb **0.1–20s**. Effects can be switched individually. These are within this algorithm,not a global freely assignable chain or28simultaneous effects. | **Factory / user / total memory:** **100 writable locations1–100 +28 protected presets101–128 =128total**. Article lists **SYSTEM–No.100** as the range andwarns **all USER information will be lost**. It does not fully explain per-selection behavior or establish a protected settings-only reset;preserve the stated range rather than inventing one.<br>**Memory / power-on recall andediting:** Power-on recalls the previously used program. This is distinct from automatic recall of an already stored program. | **Synth / acoustic:** Vocoder requirements are documented below;onboard synthesis andacoustic simulation remainunverified.<br>**Synth / vocoder requirements:** **Seven frequency bands** process external microphone audio against a sustained keyboard signal. | [HeadRush Prime](HEADRUSH.md#prime) | **Blocks / routing:** **14 available blocks**, DSP-limited. Dual-guitar and guitar/vocal paths allocate **seven per input**; other guitar-only split routings exist. Repeated block types allowed subject to CPU. A dry-mic output path is not fourteen additional vocal FX. PR-M pp.14–17; PR-S<br>**Prime — category-count qualifications:** The current product list prints these category headers: HeadRush amps **53**, ReValver amps **44**, ReValver cabinet microphones **31**; vocal **7**, overdrive **16**, distortion/fuzz **11**, EQ **9**, compressor **6**, delay **14**, reverb **7**, chorus **7**, phaser/flanger **10**, vib/trem/rotary **6**, wah/filter **8**, synth **4**, rhythmic **2**, pitch **7**, volume/dynamics **5**, utility **3**. These are **published header counts, not a reconciled installed-library census**. Overdrive visibly lists **18 names beneath its 16 header**; pitch lists eight rows with **Octaves duplicated**. Firmware 5.1 adds Graphic Bass EQ, but the displayed EQ list needs reconciliation. Do not silently sum these into a definitive current total. Cabinet/mic/IR combinations are separate from independent effects. PR-P, PR-R | **Memory:** FAQ states **50 preloaded factory rigs**; firmware-current installed content and numerical total rig/user storage ceiling unverified. Rigs, block presets, scenes, setlists, loops and captures are distinct objects. MIDI program numbers 1–128 are not proof of only 128 stored rigs. PR-F, PR-M | **Synth / acoustic:** Current Prime list explicitly includes **Hybrid Synth, Electric Piano and DB-33 Organ**; Ambi-Verb is also classified under Synth. **Acoustic Sim** and Acoustic Pre are separate EQ-category entries. Do not equate the acoustic preamp or an IR with the simulator. PR-P<br>**Vocal:** Antares Auto-Tune, De-Esser, Vocal Distortion, Doubler, Harmony, Stutter and Vocoder are separately listed; mic path and routing qualifications above apply. PR-P, PR-S | **IR / captures:** Third-party IR loading, 1024/2048-sample processing; FAQ accepts **WAV/AIFF up to 192 kHz/32-bit** and two IRs in a block. Processing length differs from accepted source-file rate/depth. Exact conversion rules/slot ceiling unresolved. Proprietary onboard **Amp Cloner**, HeadRush/ReValver clones and SuperClones are separate from NAM; SuperClone assembly is a desktop software workflow. PR-F, PR-M, PR-R<br>**Native / converted NAM:** **Native NAM A1/A2**, not a conversion-only claim. 5.1 documented one A1 Standard/Lite/Feather/Nano or A2 Full/Lite per rig; **5.2 allows two A2 Full, or four A2 Lite across two NAM blocks**, including doubling modes. Do not apply the newer A2 limits to every A1 architecture. PR-R |

DIRECT MONITOR resets ON at startup and cannot be saved. Its tempo changes MASTER BPM. This is a metronome, not evidence of a drum-pattern library. Looper remains unverified.<br>**Tuner / live:** Reference 435–445 Hz; MUTE silences output, BYPASS removes effects, THRU retains the current effect sound while tuning. These mode names do not establish analog true-bypass circuitry.<br>**USB audio / routing:** [Official parameter guide, p. 34](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), indexed original text: PRIMARY sends effects; its computer return mixes at the final stage. SECONDARY sends dry input; its return enters the effect-chain beginning for reamping. Both outputs are available together. Four recording channels are corroborated below; exact playback count remains unverified.<br>**Looper / I/O exclusions:** Shared document explicitly marks phrase-loop, send/return and amp-control entries **GT-100 only**. Those controls do not establish corresponding GT-001 capabilities. |

Its p.3 specifies **two A2 Full captures or four A2 Lite captures across two NAM blocks**, running natively. Reconciliation remains pending. The seven named vocal choices include correction,de-essing,distortion,doubling,harmony,stutter,vocoder; they are not seven extra independent microphone-routing blocks. |

| [MOOER GS1000](MOOER.md#gs1000) | **Inputs:** One 6.35mm mono GUITAR IN/INPUT L for guitar/bass; one 6.35mm/XLR MIC IN/INPUT R combo for microphone or instrument/line. TS cables provide unbalanced operation. **No XLR main outputs**<br>**FX loop:** One 6.35mm stereo TRS SEND andone RETURN, unbalanced stereo; TRS-to-dualTS breakout for stereo external effects. Serial/parallel, send/return levels, movable positions, four-cable/extended-I/O configurations<br>**Headphones / auxiliary:** 3.5mm stereo PHONES. No separate analog AUX input in complete rear inventory; Bluetooth audio for accompaniment<br>**Control / data:** 6.35mm TRS EXP; single 3.5mm TRS MIDI port configured **IN or OUT**, not two concurrent physical ports; USB-C for USB MIDI, audio, editor/data; wireless F4 support<br>**I/O / wireless footswitch ceiling:** **Only one F4 wireless footswitch can connect at a time.** Pairing is required initially or after factory reset; previously paired devices reconnect automatically when powered and in range. This limit does not establish the Bluetooth-audio connection ceiling.<br>**I/O / versioned fixes:** **v1.2.3** fixes brief dropout during preset changes with Bluetooth/USB playback; **v1.2.7** fixes balanced-output issues without specifying their electrical cause. Neither proves arbitrary gapless switching or resolves the regional output wording conflict.<br>**I/O / reamp input persistence:** Reamp requires **serial chain mode** and fixes the input source to **USB** until Normal mode is restored. The manual warns it can boot again in Reamp mode, leaving the guitar input silent. Return to Normal after reamping; this is routing behavior, not hardware failure or USB bus-power evidence.<br>**I/O / input electrical limits:** Guitar and RETURN: **1 MΩ, maximum +10 dBu**. Microphone combo: **2.4 kΩ**; its **+10 dBu** maximum is explicitly qualified for the **quarter-inch input**, not established for XLR.<br>**I/O / output electrical limits:** Main outputs: **600 Ω, maximum +16 dBu**. SEND: **510 Ω, maximum +12 dBu**. PHONES: **6 Ω, maximum +16 dBu**. Output impedance is not a recommended headphone-load rating.<br>**I/O / converter ratings:** ADC: **24-bit/44.1 kHz**, dynamic range **114 dB**. DAC: dynamic range **115 dB**, signal-to-noise **115 dB**. Both list **20 Hz–20 kHz, +0/−1 dB** response. The manual does not state the measurement weighting here; USB rates remain separate.<br>**GS1000 — complete connections:** The manual explicitly shows instrument and microphone entering independently processed chains. This establishes live mic-path membership in ALL-VOCAL without claiming a specialized singer harmony engine. [Original manual](https://media.rockboard.de/Artikell_PDF/Manual_PDF/Mooer/EN/ME_GS1000_Manual_EN.pdf) and [regional product](https://mooer.jp/products/mooer-gs1000). | **Power / battery / USB:** **DC 9V 2A center-negative**, supplied adapter. Standard version has no integrated operating battery; Li's 9V 3A adapter, 7.4V 4750mAh battery and conditional six-hour rating must not be copied here. USB-bus operation unknown<br>**Supply:** DC input; front power pushbutton; rear 48Vtoggle. No speaker-power output | **Desktop editor:** **MOOER Studio Windows 10+ and macOS 10.13+** in current exact-model download page; original manual says macOS 10.11+, retained as dated conflict. Full parameter/chain editing, preset management, IR/GNR/GIR transfer, backup, loop audio import/export and firmware updates<br>**Mobile software:** Original MOOER Cloud supports iOS 11+/Android 5.0+ over Bluetooth for preset/sample upload/download; these are historical app requirements, not confirmed current minima. Do not equate cloud exchange with a full mobile parameter editor<br>**Drivers / USB audio:** Separate Windows ASIO driver; original Mac audio plug-and-play. **2in/2out, 24bit44.1–192kHz USB**, wet/dry channel selection, hardware/software monitor mix and reamp.192kHz USB support is separate from44.1kHz internal ADC and IR specs<br>**Editor / Cloud NAM handling:** Chinese **Cloud1.6.1,2026-07-28** adds NAM-A2 file handling for GS1000/GE1000. This does not establish full mobile parameter editing or expand hardware native support beyond A2 Lite. Cloud1.7.0’s stem pan/transpose/speed features are app functions, not new pedal effect blocks.<br>**Editor / backup scope:** Backup instructions explicitly include presets,loadedGNR/GIR/IR andglobalsettings. Looperaudio is not enumerated there; do not assume this backup preserves loops. Separate loop import/export exists; format andpower-offpersistence remain unverified. | **Routing architecture:** Five templates: Serial, Dual, Split, Mix, Parallel. Independent input/output nodes, movable split/mix; dual chains can separately process instrument and microphone. Mix cannot precede split or share its position<br>**Scene / sub-patch control:** **3 SubPatches per preset** with module states and parameter mappings, plus stomp/tap/single/multiple/A–B CTRL functions. F4 wireless/MIDI can extend control<br>**Looper / drums:** **480 seconds/8minutes stereo**, explicitly stated by the [Chinese exact-model page](https://cn.mooeraudio.com/product/94.html); overdub, undo/redo, auto-record threshold, independent levels, drum sync, count-in and loop import/export. Serial/parallel, send/return levels, movable positions, four-cable/extended-I/O configurations<br>**Looper / synchronization boundary:** DRUM SYNC uses a one-bar count-in, except when AUTO REC is active or drums are already playing. The first loop is rounded to whole measures: a remainder below half a bar is discarded; above half a bar recording continues to the next bar. Exactly-half behavior is not specified.<br>**Looper / leaving the screen:** Closing Groove Station **does not stop playing loops or drums**. Reopen it to stop them; F4 can separately control DRUM ON/OFF. Screen exit is not storage or power-cycle persistence evidence.<br>**Looper / undo and deletion:** FootswitchA cyclesREC/PLAY/DUB; holdUNDO,holdagainREDO after multiple layers. FootswitchB tapstops,holddeletes the entire recording. Undo is not full deletion.<br>**GS1000 — dated firmware and Cloud changes:** Checked 2026-10-18 against [global release notes](https://www.mooeraudio.com/Downloads_xq/7.html) and [Chinese release notes](https://cn.mooeraudio.com/Downloads_xq/5.html). Listed releases are not proof of an offered unit’s installed version; nothing downloaded.<br>**GS1000 — loop synchronization and reamp routing:** Checked 2026-10-18 against the [original manufacturer manual](https://media.rockboard.de/Artikell_PDF/Manual_PDF/Mooer/EN/ME_GS1000_Manual_EN.pdf), printed pp47–51.<br>**GS1000 — loop synchronization and reamp routing:** Loop import/export is documented elsewhere, but no supported loop-audio format was established in these inspected instructions. The existing IR WAV format must not be reused as a looper-file specification. |

Guitar-thru table prints**270/540Ω balanced/unbalanced**;retained in source order,not silently reversed. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp196–197<br>**MIDI / USB source and timing:** Reference p164:**Source selectsUSB orDIN MIDI IN**,withseparate input/output channel settings;MIDI Tempo follows external clock. Input/OutputFilters specify messages to suppress,not enable(p165). Manual p87 warns DAW USB-audio/MIDI alignment may require MIDI offset compensation;no numeric latency established. This documents USB MIDI reception,not a DIN-to-USB merge/router orUSB-host controller support. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp87,164–165<br>**I/O / NaturalPlay guidance sources:** Introductionp48 names guitar,MIDI keyboard,AUX andMonitor In for Harmony/HardTune guidance. Systemp175 listsAuto,Guitar,MIDI,AUX,RoomSense;Auto text also namesUSB. RoomSense may blend with other guidance andcan work alone with sufficient chord information;AUX typeLive/Tracks improves detection(p176). These differing lists do not establish a complete automatic priority order or USB-audio chord-detection route. — Evidence: [Reference pp48,175–176](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) | **Power / battery / USB:** External supply mains100–240V50–60Hz;consumption<14W. Quick guide explicitly specifies12V DC input with supplied adapter. Current rating,polarity,battery andUSB bus power unverified. **Consumption conflict:** older reference <14W versus indexed current quick guide <12W;not treated as measured draw orresolved hardware revision. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) p198; [Quick guide](https://manualzilla.com/doc/6743797/voicelive-3-extreme---tc) p7; [Indexed current guide](https://mediadl.musictribe.com/media/PLM/data/docs/P0DEG/QSG_TH_0726-AAY_VOICELIVE-3-EXTREME_WW.pdf) pp122–123 | **USB audio / MIDI:** Stereo2-in/2-out,16-bit48kHz, generic Windows/OS X drivers. **USB audio and backing tracks mutually exclusive.** Stereo input feeds XLR/headphones; DAW Tracking return feeds headphones only. DAW Insert processes left via vocal and right via guitar path. Tracking-only output option sends dry vocal left and processed guitar right;other modes lock it. DIN MIDI present;manualp164 explicitly selects **USB orhardware MIDI IN as MIDI receive source**;additional routing limits below. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp85–87,198<br>**Editor / platforms:** VoiceSupport for Windows/Mac OS X: preset libraries,drag/drop management,firmware upgrades. Full patch-editor scope andcurrent OS compatibility unresolved;device USB audio drivers are separate. — Evidence: [Quick guide](https://manualzilla.com/doc/6743797/voicelive-3-extreme---tc) p3<br>**Direct USB recording:** FAT32 flash drive in host port;stereo main-output mirror includes backing tracks/loops. Maximum120min per recording;each start/stop creates a new file. Distinct from computer USB audio/backing-track exclusion. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) p84<br>**Editor / MIDI configuration prerequisite:** Reference p167 requires **SysEx ID0 for VoiceSupport firmware updates/preset management**. MIDITranspose shifts incomingnotes up/down up to4octaves,falling back to nearest usableoctave outsideMIDI range(p165);SplitNote C−1…G9 selectsabove/below keyboard region(p166). These are incoming-note controls,not guitar-audio transposition. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp165–167 | **Loop / backing-track imports:** Loops: WAV16/24-bit44.1/48kHz;24/48 fastest. No MP3/AAX/FLAC loop import. 4/4,power-of-two bar lengths and no rendered tail for sync/tempo detection. Backing tracks use internal16-bit48kHz and can convert MP3/other WAV formats; do not conflate with loop imports. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp73,143–144<br>**Audio routing / loop storage:** Each loop track contains stereo vocal and stereo guitar sub-tracks;imports select destination. Main XLR includes guitar when guitar outs unused;one connected guitar out automatically gives mono. Guitar Unity Gain ignores Mix level butstill responds to guitar-volume expression;Variable Level follows Mix. Speaker simulation globally switchable. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp29–30,146,162–163<br>**Loop triggers / MIDI sync:** Each slot stores A/B/C;parallel playback uses A+B orA+C,notthree concurrent tracks. **SERIAL sync mode instead plays A,B,C independently,one at a time**,waiting for each loop to finish before changing tracks;order is freely selectable(p140). Triggers A–H choose slot/track;assignments saved per preset ormade global with Global Triggers. Triggering another loop recalls its tempo andchanges tempo-dependent effects. MIDI tempo slave only;no clock generation. Existing loops do not time-stretch after tempo changes. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp72,131,151–153<br>**Backing-track automation / live:** Records vocal/guitar effect footswitches,HIT/ALL HIT andSteps within preset;continuous parameter changes excluded. A playing backing track with a sequence prevents preset changes until pause/stop. Switch-3/6/STEP track-control assignments global. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp79–80<br>**Looper / drums / scenes:** Loop tracks A/B/C,8min maximum per track; A plays while B/C swap. Last-overdub Undo/redo. 50 slots (1–50);aggregate audio-storage time,drums,scenes andtrails unresolved. UTIL Save stores loop audio/settings in slot,notpreset. Steps remain distinct. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) p105,195<br>**Live / HIT state and saving:** Reference pp60–64:**ALL HIT controls both vocal/guitar layers**. DefaultDynamic HIT incorporates manual effect-state changes;Snapshot recalls saved HIT states regardless of currently active effects. **Snapshot requires saving both HIT-off andHIT-on states separately**. Effect control options areOff,On,HITOn,HITOff(p67);not a new set of freely assignable scenes. Unsaved preset edits disappear on preset change(p68). — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp60–68<br>**Live / tempo and global effects:** Taptempo allows**1BPM fine adjustment**;GlobalTempo makes selectedpresets follow the tappedtempo(pp65–66). GlobalEffects pulls effect settings from a designated GlobalPreset(default491);changing/deleting that source affects dependent presets(p69). This is shared parameter sourcing,not proof of gapless preset switching orspillover. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp65–69<br>**Live / Talk-Tuner and layer switching:** Reference pp22–23:**Talk/Tuner mutes guitar output andbypasses vocal effects**;PresetUp/Down can still select sounds whiletalking. Layer tap switches vocal/guitar control;hold enters/exits loop/backing-track layer. These control layers do not imply the other audio layer stops processing. — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp22–23<br>**Looper / storage check and import preparation:** Hold **GENRE two seconds** to open About; **FREE LOOP STORAGE** reports remaining time across all slots/tracks;any button exits(p142). This is a remaining-capacity display,not evidence of nominal aggregate capacity. Loop import requires a **FAT32** USB flash drive and **VL3_Loops** folder;files placed in its root appear in Backing Track import instead. Optional filenames **SLOT#_TRACK_VOC_userinfo** or **SLOT#_TRACK_GTR_userinfo** prefill destination slot,track andvocal/guitar sub-track(pp144–145). — Evidence: [Manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/12745534/tc-helicon-voicelive-3-extreme-owner-s-manual) pp140,142,144–145<br>**Looper / SMART and QUANTIZE:** SMART trims/extends loops andrequiresB/C to be bar-length multiples ofA,no shorter. QUANTIZE snaps to the nearest beat without that length adjustment;shorterB can repeat withinA. FREE uses independent loop lengths without synchronization. These are timing modes,not additional loop tracks.<br>**Looper / metronome state:** MET MUTE silences the click while retaining tempo/LoopAssist. MET OFF disables those synchronization features. Main andheadphone metronome levels are independently adjustable,allowing a performer-only click.<br>**Looper / tempo lock:** Recording with metronomeOn locks that slot’s tempo;change requires deleting the loop orusing another slot. Recording with metronomeOff permits later tempo changes,not proof of audio time-stretching. |

MIX guitar-level adjustment restores guitar to MIC OUT while retaining THRU;replug THRU or Guitar IN re-mutes. Anti-feedback filtering is mic-only;guitar phase invert is separate. Unit Talk/Tuner bypasses vocal effects except Tone and mutes guitar;mic-control Talk leaves guitar active without tuner. MIX held5s locks/mutes but preserves AUX playback. Guitar takes chord-detection priority over AUX. Hold Headphone while inserting AUX to exclude it from main output; replug or power resets. Startup Harmony/Headphone/MIX temporarily assigns the knob to harmony/headphone/guitar level; without guitar, guitar-level mode falls back to main level. Prior main level remains; restart in default mode to change it. Looper/drums/scenes/trails unverified;controller labels alone prove none. — Evidence: [Official manual p4](https://mediadl.musictribe.com/media/PLM/data/docs/P0DED/PERFORM-VG_M_EN.pdf);[Manual pp11–14](https://device.report/m/41c9e7a9189b970b66abce1cc7fec8af176268ec87e2f75f7611498e19d053bd)<br>**Live / Talk and reset operation:** Hold MIX2s for Talk/Tuner; tuner and guitar-level control require a connected guitar. Factory reset uses Vocal Echo + Harmony held at power-on. This reset procedure is not the volatile startup knob reassignment. Documentation only; no device operated. — Evidence: [Rev1.0 pp12,14](https://device.report/m/41c9e7a9189b970b66abce1cc7fec8af176268ec87e2f75f7611498e19d053bd)<br>**Routing and temporary control assignments:** Default rotary function is main output level. Previous main level remains in the background;changing it requires restarting in default mode. These assignments are not stored presets.<br>**Routing and temporary control assignments:** Hold Headphone while inserting the1/8-inch AUX cable to exclude AUX from main output;replug or power resets exclusion. Guitar outranks AUX for chord detection.<br>**Routing and temporary control assignments:** A remote harmony-style switch turns its style on when harmony is off,toggles off when pressed again,or changes style while remaining on when a different assigned switch is pressed. Footswitch reassignment excludes SET,48V,Headphone Level andLock/Mute;Talk is assignable. These style switches do not establish simultaneous extra harmony blocks. [Rev1.0 manual pp13–14](https://device.report/m/41c9e7a9189b970b66abce1cc7fec8af176268ec87e2f75f7611498e19d053bd). |

SET+component erases thatpart. p20 describes externalMIDIclock;appendixp27 saysMIDI Tempo,notMIDIClock:unresolved conflict. Duration/storage/scenes pending. Vocal sample islost atpoweroff. Headphone level cannotbe adjusted until loop iserased. Flange affects loop audio/drums;sidechain pump affects audio but not drums;LPF/HPF affects both. Other transducer styles exclude drums. — Evidence: [QSG](https://mediadl.musictribe.com/media/PLM/data/docs/P0DEC/QSG_TH_0726-AAR_PERFORM-VE_WW.pdf);[Manual pp19–24,27](https://www.fullcompass.com/common/files/32341-TCHeliconPERFORMVEUserManual.pdf)<br>**Routing qualifications:** Rev.1.2 p24 contradicts itself: AUX chord detection is first denied, then AUX is described as a key/scale guide. Holding Headphone while inserting AUX excludes it from mains; unplug/replug or power resets this setting. Headphones retain AUX. RoomSense practice requires headphones and no external mic, and mutes mains. These are documented routing restrictions, not guitar multi-effects capability. [Manual pp24–25](https://www.fullcompass.com/common/files/32341-TCHeliconPERFORMVEUserManual.pdf) |

Hit changes effect on/off states only,not effect parameter settings (reference pp.27–29). — Evidence: [Reference](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Output routing / USB recording:** USB Out controls overall main mix sent to computer;separate multitrack outputs not established. Lead Mute removes dry voice for effects-return use. Headphone level independent ofAnalog Out (reference pp.5,59). — Evidence: [Reference](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Tone / Talk operation:** Tone processing remains active inTalk;global Pitch Cor Amt switches off inTalk. Normal Tone default;four active tonal/dynamic variants eachwith gated/no-gate versions,plusOff. Chromatic Pitch Cor Amt needs no scale andis distinct fromextreme Hardtune (reference pp.57–58). — Evidence: [Reference](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Looper / drums / scenes:** Six loop layers share first-loop length; unlimited overdubs. Undo only last operation (overdub,Clear,2x); Reverse/Mixdown cannot be undone. Disabling Undo doubles available recording time. 2x consumes memory for other layers andbecomes unavailable after recording another slot. Metronome quantizes recording/play andsupports auto re-sync. Duration/persistence/drums remain unverified;Hit is alternate preset variation,not inferred scene count (reference pp.36,41–45). — Evidence: [Quick Guide](https://manualzz.com/doc/6755730/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-quick-start-guide); [Reference](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Looper routing / MIDI:** Loop input choices: Lead+Guitar (default,excludes Aux),Guitar,Lead orAux. MIDI Clock via USB/DIN;syncs beats,not bars;MTC unsupported. Queued Mode waits for loop point when entering/exiting overdub. Feedback defaults98%;below100% gradually reduces retained audio during successive overdubs (reference pp.48,64–66). — Evidence: [Reference](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Loop FX / metronome:** Four loop-playback choices: Filter,Slow Speed,Squeeze Select,Squeeze Auto;one selected effect modifies all playing layers. Shots with transport running continues on release;stopped transport plays only while held. Metronome routes to all outputs orphones only;Sine,Kick Drum,HiHat are click sounds,not evidence of a rhythm-pattern library (reference pp.49–50,66). — Evidence: [Reference](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Looper / Erase versus Clear:** Reference p.39: Home-mode Record hold1s erases without confirmation or undo. Loop Tools Erase prompts first. Erase removes loop information including length; Clear removes the selected layer's audio/overdubs but retains timing and is undoable. No device operation performed. — Evidence: [Reference pp.39,43](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual)<br>**Looper / Mixdown target:** Reference p.51: Mixdown consolidates layers into a selected slot and clears source layers; cannot be undone. Audible target content joins the mix; a muted target's previous content is overwritten. This frees slots, not proof of extra recording duration or persistent storage. — Evidence: [Reference p.51](https://manualzz.com/doc/9506742/tc-helicon-voicelive-touch-2-reference-manual) |

A start/end range follows the same sample-rate-dependent maximum as PRESET Freeze. No PRESET LOOP/LOOP NUM controls are documented for these simpler variants. — Evidence: [Manual pp78–81](https://data.yamaha.com/files/download/other_assets/1/323961/spx2000_en_om_f0.pdf)<br>**Live / sample erasure:** Sample data is erased by record-ready,Undo,Input Mode,Recall,Compare,input-source or clock-source changes; external clock-frequency changes also erase it when slaved. Storing a program is not proof of persistent recorded audio. — Evidence: [Manual p80](https://data.yamaha.com/files/download/other_assets/1/323961/spx2000_en_om_f0.pdf)<br>**Looper / drums / live:** PRESET Freeze is a short mono-input/stereo-output sampler: maximum2970.5ms at44.1/88.2kHz or2729.2ms at48/96kHz. Manual/automatic recording; panel,input-level or MIDI-note playback triggering. START/END/LOOP points;0–100 loop repeats;±12-semitone playback pitch. Reversed START/END enables reverse playback. Entering record-ready erases the previous sample; power-off erases recorded audio. This does not establish a persistent overdub looper. CLASSIC Freeze A/B differences below. Drums,scenes and patch spillover unverified. — Evidence: [Manual pp77–81](https://data.yamaha.com/files/download/other_assets/1/323961/spx2000_en_om_f0.pdf)<br>**Live / bypass on recall:** Recalling an effect **turns BYPASS off**. Bypass passes input without effect processing, but the manual does not establish relay/analog true bypass. Do not assume a muted or bypassed transition persists across recall. — Evidence: [Manual pp8,12](https://data.yamaha.com/files/download/other_assets/1/323961/spx2000_en_om_f0.pdf)<br>**Live / shared tempo:** TEMPO is **shared by all effects and is not stored/recalled**. On recall, saved delay/modulation-frequency values are retained despite a different current tempo; subsequent tempo edits recalculate them when synchronized. NOTE/TEMPO ranges are limited by effect delay/frequency limits, even with SYNC off. — Evidence: [Manual p15](https://data.yamaha.com/files/download/other_assets/1/323961/spx2000_en_om_f0.pdf)<br>**SPX2000 — clock and logical MIDI-port routing:** Checked 2026-10-17 against the own manufacturer manual. |

Input and output impedances remain distinct.<br>**I/O / digital-lock fallback:** For a digital insert, the connected interface supplies master clock. If digital input is selected but no valid signal arrives within5seconds, DIGI IN flashes and M350 returns to internal clock and analog operation while continuing to seek digital input. A selected digital input therefore does not guarantee a permanently digital signal path.<br>**I/O / MIDI channel and SysEx ID:** Receive channel1–16, O for all channels, or OF to ignore incoming MIDI; factory/reset default ischannel1. SysEx ID follows the selected MIDI channel. LOAD confirms the menu choice. These addressing options do not establish editor operating-system support.<br>**I/O / input-gain control:** INPUT controls both L/R channels:analog gain spans **−10 to+14dB**,digital **−18 to+6dB**,each0dB at center. These are gain ranges,not maximum input levels or dBu nominal sensitivities;the electrical ratings remain separately documented. Unequal source levels require adjustment upstream rather than separate L/R gain knobs. | **Power:** **100–240VAC50–60Hz auto;less than15W**. Internal supply;DCpolarityN/A. MIDI control/bulkdump documented;notUSB audio.<br>**Editor / firmware-display distinction:** PRESET ON/OFF held with UP/DOWN selects **AP** application or **Fr** front software version for service. These identify hardware software versions,not AU/VST editor releases or supported desktop operating systems. Existing cutsheet editor claim remains separately qualified.<br>**Editor / MIDI backup operation:** Hold PRESET ON/OFF about2.5seconds, select Bulk Dump with arrows, then LOAD sends presets to a recording MIDI device. Receive is automatic unless MIDI Receive Channel isOF. This is backup support, not proof of AU/VST editor compatibility.<br>**Editor / dated feature provenance:** [TC Summer2007 catalogue,p11](https://service-tcgroup.tcelectronic.com/media/Touring_Install_DE.pdf) describes a bundled VST editor with parameter/preset automation and realtime control;the [English cutsheet](https://service-tcgroup.tcelectronic.com/media/M350_Cutsheet_web.pdf) also lists AU. This dates the capability claim;recent indexing/upload labels are not new editor releases. Installer availability,OS versions,plugin bitness and current host compatibility remain unverified. | **Looper / drums / live:** Looper/drums/scenes unknown;tap/MIDIclock. Bypass mutes indualinput,passes signal inserial.<br>**Routing / parallel mono setup:** **Analog input only**:Dual Input switch IN,left input connected,right empty. Left is internally split to both engines,which share stereo outputs. Mixer aux use requires MIX100%wet. This is not a freely selectable digital parallel routing mode.<br>**Routing / serial versus dual input:** Serial passes Delay/Effects then Reverb. Dual Input processes two separate sources through the respective engines with common outputs;it does not provide isolated output pairs per source.<br>**Editor / firmware-display distinction:** PRESET ON/OFF held with UP/DOWN selects **AP** application or **Fr** front software version for service. These identify hardware software versions,not AU/VST editor releases or supported desktop operating systems. Existing cutsheet editor claim remains separately qualified.<br>**Routing / standalone A/D conversion:** With analog input feeding S/PDIF output, M350 supplies master clock and the receiving interface must follow it. Both effect selectors Off provide conversion without added effects. Using Bypass for this purpose requires Serial routing; Dual Input bypass mutes instead. This is S/PDIF conversion, not USB audio.<br>**Editor / MIDI backup operation:** Hold PRESET ON/OFF about2.5seconds, select Bulk Dump with arrows, then LOAD sends presets to a recording MIDI device. Receive is automatic unless MIDI Receive Channel isOF. This is backup support, not proof of AU/VST editor compatibility.<br>**Live / panel versus stored state:** Preset Off processes according to front-control positions. Returning to Preset On keeps that panel-derived sound until LOAD recalls the blinking stored preset. The mode name does not mean audio bypass; physical knob positions can differ from recalled settings.<br>**Editor / dated feature provenance:** [TC Summer2007 catalogue,p11](https://service-tcgroup.tcelectronic.com/media/Touring_Install_DE.pdf) describes a bundled VST editor with parameter/preset automation and realtime control;the [English cutsheet](https://service-tcgroup.tcelectronic.com/media/M350_Cutsheet_web.pdf) also lists AU. This dates the capability claim;recent indexing/upload labels are not new editor releases. Installer availability,OS versions,plugin bitness and current host compatibility remain unverified.<br>**Looper / delay timing limits:** Cutsheet advertises **5seconds of delay**. [Rev1.1 manual,printedp24](https://cdn.mediavalet.com/aunsw/musictribe/oexFf_5Xrkic0GfCn2XAEw/j6PZVTEe-UynlIA4ETJYOA/Original/tc_electronic_m350_manual_english.pdf) gives TIMING **0.5–2×** the tapped interval and power-up tempo120BPM;range depends on algorithm,with Slapback shorter. Do not assign5seconds to every delay type or confuse this with phrase-loop recording.<br>**Routing / effect mix restrictions:** In Serial routing,compressor/de-esser mix is fixed100%;chorus/flanger/phaser/tremolo mix is fixed50%. MIX adjusts delay/reverb,with BALANCE adjusting their relative contribution. In Dual S/R,MIX controls overall wet/dry for both engines. These routing-dependent rules do not establish arbitrary independent wet/dry control for every algorithm.<br>**M350 — routing details and service-version display:** Checked 2026-10-18 in the [same Rev1.1 manufacturer manual,pp12,22–23](https://cdn.mediavalet.com/aunsw/musictribe/oexFf_5Xrkic0GfCn2XAEw/j6PZVTEe-UynlIA4ETJYOA/Original/tc_electronic_m350_manual_english.pdf). Original text only;no local download or hardware operation.<br>**M350 — routing details and service-version display:** Editor installers/current OS compatibility,control-button count,display diagonal/touch and qualified used/new IDR evidence remain unresolved.<br>**M350 — routing-dependent mix and input-gain ranges:** Checked 2026-10-18 from the [manufacturer Rev1.1/SW1.00 manual,printed pp16–17](https://cdn.mediavalet.com/aunsw/musictribe/oexFf_5Xrkic0GfCn2XAEw/j6PZVTEe-UynlIA4ETJYOA/Original/tc_electronic_m350_manual_english.pdf),original text only. All amounts are dated asking observations, **not a verified used-to-new range, completed sale or checkout stock**. Sold-out, voucher, region, bundle, condition and cached-access qualifications belong to the offer and remain visible. Missing IDR is not estimated from USD. A retail-labelled offer is not independently confirmed new condition.

## Scope and provenance

Instrument views overlap and must not be summed. All 349 active models count once in `research-state.json`; the six explicitly excluded archives never enter these tables. Former/donated eligible devices remain without private receipt/order/payment details. Root brand records take precedence where consolidated; legacy sources remain for full evidence until a checked lossless merge. No GitHub workflow is used.
